## PART 1 — Token optimization (POST-LLMLingua SOTA)

### LLMLingua-replacement EXPLICIT VERDICT

**The operator is correct: microsoft/LLMLingua is outdated for 2026 Claude Code.** Primary-source evidence:

- microsoft/LLMLingua live github description: "[EMNLP'23, ACL'24] To speed up LLMs' inference..." — academic 2023-2024 framing; positions as *external compressor before sending to API* — pre-server-side-compaction era.
- LLMLingua is third-party LOSSY token-dropper sitting outside model server. Anthropic API now ships **server-side primitives** that subsume the use case AND are LOSSLESS (vs LLMLingua's lossy token-dropping).

**The 2026 SOTA replacement is a STACK of 5 Anthropic-native primitives + codebase packer + cross-session memory tool**, NOT a single repo.

[7m[0m## Overview[0m
[7m[0m

  y Iteration**: Current memory is just a recor
  d of user interactions, lacking Agent-related
>  task memory.

### The Open[7mViking[0m Solution[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m*[0m
> *Open[7mViking[0m** is an open-source **Context Dat[0m
[7m[0m  abase** designed specifically for AI Agents.[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m  [0m
[7m[0mWe aim to define a minimalist context intera[0m
  ction paradigm for Agents, allowing developer
  s to completely say goodbye to the hassle of 
> context management. Open[7mViking[0m abandons the f[0m
[7m[0m  ragmented vector storage model of traditional[0m
[7m[0m   RAG and innovatively adopts a **"file system[0m
   paradigm"** to unify the structured organiza
  tion of memories, resources, and skills neede
> d by Agents.

With Open[7mViking[0m, developers can[0m
[7m[0m   build an Agent's brain just like managing lo[0m
[7m[0m  cal files:[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m- **Filesystem Management Paradig[0m
  ory, making the Agent smarter with use.

***

### 3. Environment Configuration

##
  ## Quick Setup for Local Models (Ollama)

If 
> you want to run Open[7mViking[0m with local models [0m
[7m[0m  via [Ollama](https://ollama.ai), the interact[0m
[7m[0m  ive setup wizard handles everything automatic[0m
> ally:

```bash
open[7mviking[0m-server init[0m
[7m[0m```[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0mTh[0m
[7m[0m  e wizard will:[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m- Detect and install Ollama i[0m
[7m[0m  f needed[0m
[7m[0m- Recommend and pull suitable embedd[0m
  te a ready-to-use `ov.conf` configuration fil
  e

To validate your setup at any time:

```ba
> sh
open[7mviking[0m-server doctor[0m
[7m[0m```[0m

### [7mViking[0mBot Quick Start[0m

> d for most users)

> ]"

[7m[0m   (for development)[0m
[7m[0muv pip install -e ".[bot]"[0m
> 

# Start Open[7mViking[0m server with Bot enabled[0m

[7m[0m  rminal, start interactive chat[0m
[7m[0mov chat[0m
[7m[0m```[0m

  iled step-by-step guide to get you started qu
> ickly.

## Core Concepts

After running the firs
  t example, let's dive into the design philoso
> phy of Open[7mViking[0m. These five core concepts c[0m
[7m[0m  orrespond one-to-one with the solutions menti[0m
[7m[0m  oned earlier, together building a complete co[0m
  system. Whether it's memories, resources, or 
  capabilities, they are mapped to virtual dire
> ctories under the `[7mviking[0m://` protocol, each [0m
[7m[0m  with a unique URI.[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0mThis paradigm gives Agent[0m
[7m[0m  s unprecedented context manipulation capabili[0m
  orms context management from vague semantic m
  atching into intuitive, traceable "file opera
> tions". Learn more: [[7mViking[0m URI](./docs/en/co[0m
> ncepts/04-[7mviking[0m-uri.md) | [Context Types](./[0m
[7m[0m  docs/en/concepts/02-context-types.md)[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m```[0m
[7m[0mvi[0m
[7m[0m  king://[0m
[7m[0m├── resources/              # R[0m
  esources: project docs, repos, web pages, etc
  .
│   ├── my_project/
│   │   ├
> ── docs/
│   │   │   ├── [7mapi[0m/[0m
[7m[0m  [0m
[7m[0m│   │   │   └── tutorials/[0m
[7m[0m│  [0m
[7m[0m   │   └── src/[0m
[7m[0m│   └── ...[0m
[7m[0m├[0m
  t into a prompt all at once is not only expen
  sive but also prone to exceeding model window
> s and introducing noise. Open[7mViking[0m automatic[0m
[7m[0m  ally processes context into three levels upon[0m
[7m[0m   writing:[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m- **L0 (Abstract)**: A one-sentenc[0m
  ading by the Agent when absolutely necessary.
  

Learn more: [Context Layers](./docs/en/conc
> epts/03-context-layers.md)

```
[7mviking[0m://reso[0m
[7m[0m  urces/my_project/[0m
[7m[0m├── .abstract        [0m
[7m[0m         # L0 Layer: Abstract (~100 tokens) - Q[0m

**Q3 Native CC Path Corrections**

- `mksglu/context-mode`: verified native marketplace via `.claude-plugin/marketplace.json`; correction: license is Elastic-2.0 in installed plugin, not MIT.
- `yamadashy/repomix`: verified native marketplace with `repomix-mcp`, `repomix-commands`, `repomix-explorer`.
- `@arizeai/phoenix-mcp@4.0.13`: not verified. npm/web shows `@arizeai/phoenix-mcp` exists, but visible latest is `2.2.9`, Apache-2.0. A3’s `4.0.13` is false.
- `langfuse-mcp@1.2.0`: not verified. Official Langfuse path is HTTP MCP endpoint, e.g. `https://cloud.langfuse.com/api/public/mcp`, not an npm package.
- `chopratejas/headroom`: verified native marketplace exists via `.claude-plugin/marketplace.json`, plugin `headroom`.
- `agent-skills@addy-agent-skills`: verified via upstream `.claude-plugin/marketplace.json` and local cache.

**Q4 OpenViking**

A1 was too blunt. The self-host server is AGPLv3, so vendoring/running it as part of the runtime remains blocked. I did not find a managed “OpenViking Cloud SaaS API” that avoids server adoption; the README recommends deploying on Volcengine ECS, which is still self-hosting. Viable workaround: cite-only architecture and build a clean-room `viking://` interface/stub around non-AGPL incumbents. If a future managed OpenViking SaaS appears, a pure HTTP client path could be reconsidered.

**Q5 Top Duplicate Collisions**

1. `context-mode` vs installed `context-mode` plugin cache and removed standalone MCP replacement.
2. `repomix` vs installed `.mcp.json` `repomix`.
3. `phoenix-mcp` vs installed `.mcp.json` `phoenix`.
4. `ccusage/@ccusage/mcp` vs installed `.mcp.json` `ccusage`.
5. `claude-mem` / `mem0` / `supermemory` vs installed `memory` + `graphiti`; these are study-pilot alternatives, not adopt-now primitives.

**Q3 Native CC Path Corrections**

- `mksglu/context-mode`: verified native marketplace via `.claude-plugin/marketplace.json`; correction: license is Elastic-2.0 in installed plugin, not MIT.
- `yamadashy/repomix`: verified native marketplace with `repomix-mcp`, `repomix-commands`, `repomix-explorer`.
- `@arizeai/phoenix-mcp@4.0.13`: not verified. npm/web shows `@arizeai/phoenix-mcp` exists, but visible latest is `2.2.9`, Apache-2.0. A3’s `4.0.13` is false.
- `langfuse-mcp@1.2.0`: not verified. Official Langfuse path is HTTP MCP endpoint, e.g. `https://cloud.langfuse.com/api/public/mcp`, not an npm package.
- `chopratejas/headroom`: verified native marketplace exists via `.claude-plugin/marketplace.json`, plugin `headroom`.
- `agent-skills@addy-agent-skills`: verified via upstream `.claude-plugin/marketplace.json` and local cache.

**Q4 OpenViking**

A1 was too blunt. The self-host server is AGPLv3, so vendoring/running it as part of the runtime remains blocked. I did not find a managed “OpenViking Cloud SaaS API” that avoids server adoption; the README recommends deploying on Volcengine ECS, which is still self-hosting. Viable workaround: cite-only architecture and build a clean-room `viking://` interface/stub around non-AGPL incumbents. If a future managed OpenViking SaaS appears, a pure HTTP client path could be reconsidered.

**Q5 Top Duplicate Collisions**

1. `context-mode` vs installed `context-mode` plugin cache and removed standalone MCP replacement.
2. `repomix` vs installed `.mcp.json` `repomix`.
3. `phoenix-mcp` vs installed `.mcp.json` `phoenix`.
4. `ccusage/@ccusage/mcp` vs installed `.mcp.json` `ccusage`.
5. `claude-mem` / `mem0` / `supermemory` vs installed `memory` + `graphiti`; these are study-pilot alternatives, not adopt-now primitives.
